avg_similarity_score=avg_similarity,\n                avg_engagement_score=avg_engagement,\n                avg_quality_score=avg_quality,\n                processing_time_ms=processing_time_ms,\n            )\n\n            # Fetch full recommendation with items\n            result = await self._repository.get_recommendation(workspace_id, recommendation_id)\n            result[\"items\"] = items\n\n            logger.info(\n                f\"Generated {len(items)} recommendations for workspace {workspace_id}\",\n                extra={\n                    \"recommendation_id\": str(recommendation_id),\n                    \"type\": recommendation_type.value,\n                    \"processing_time_ms\": processing_time_ms,\n                },\n            )\n\n            return result\n\n        except Exception as e:\n            logger.error(f\"Failed to generate recommendations: {e}\")\n            await self._repository.update_recommendation_status(\n                workspace_id=workspace_id,\n                recommendation_id=recommendation_id,\n                status=\"failed\",\n                error_message=str(e),\n            )\n            raise\n\n    async def _generate_items(\n        self,\n        workspace_id: UUID,\n        recommendation_type: RecommendationType,\n        *,\n        client_id: UUID | None = None,\n        gallery_id: UUID | None = None,\n        source_gallery_ids: list[UUID] | None = None,\n        reference_asset_id: UUID | None = None,\n        occasion_type: str | None = None,\n        target_season: str | None = None,\n        target_theme: str | None = None,\n        limit: int = 20,\n        filter_criteria: dict[str, Any] | None = None,\n        config: RecommendationConfig = DEFAULT_CONFIG,\n    ) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:\n        \"\"\"Generate recommendation items based on type.\"\"\"\n\n        if recommendation_type == RecommendationType.SIMILAR_STYLE:\n            return await self._generate_similar_style(\n                workspace_id, reference_asset_id, limit, config\n            )\n\n        elif recommendation_type == RecommendationType.PERSONALIZED:\n            return await self._generate_personalized(\n                workspace_id, client_id, source_gallery_ids, limit, config\n            )\n\n        elif recommendation_type == RecommendationType.HERO_SHOTS:\n            return await self._generate_hero_shots(\n                workspace_id, source_gallery_ids, limit, config\n            )\n\n        elif recommendation_type == RecommendationType.TRENDING_SHOTS:\n            return await self._generate_trending(\n                workspace_id, source_gallery_ids, limit, config\n            )\n\n        elif recommendation_type == RecommendationType.SEASONAL_PICKS:\n            return await self._generate_seasonal(\n                workspace_id, target_season, source_gallery_ids, limit, config\n            )\n\n        elif recommendation_type == RecommendationType.PORTFOLIO_SELECTION:\n            return await self._generate_portfolio_selection(\n                workspace_id, client_id, gallery_id, occasion_type, limit, config\n            )\n\n        elif recommendation_type == RecommendationType.PRINT_WORTHY:\n            return await self._generate_print_worthy(\n                workspace_id, source_gallery_ids, limit, config\n            )\n\n        elif recommendation_type == RecommendationType.SOCIAL_MEDIA:\n            return await self._generate_social_media(\n                workspace_id, source_gallery_ids, limit, config\n            )\n\n        elif recommendation_type == RecommendationType.ALBUM_COVER:\n            return await self._generate_album_cover(\n                workspace_id, gallery_id, limit, config\n            )\n\n        else:\n            # Default: engagement-based selection\n            return await self._generate_by_engagement(\n                workspace_id, source_gallery_ids, limit, config\n            )\n\n    async def _generate_similar_style(\n        self,\n        workspace_id: UUID,\n        reference_asset_id: UUID | None,\n        limit: int,\n        config: RecommendationConfig,\n    ) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:\n        \"\"\"Generate recommendations similar to a reference asset.\"\"\"\n        if not reference_asset_id:\n            return []\n\n        # Get reference embedding\n        ref_data = await self._repository.get_asset_embedding(workspace_id, reference_asset_id)\n        if not ref_data or not ref_data.get(\"clip_embedding\"):\n            return []\n\n        ref_embedding = ref_data[\"clip_embedding\"]\n        if isinstance(ref_embedding, str):\n            ref_embedding = [float(x) for x in ref_embedding.strip(\"[]\").split(\",\")]\n\n        # Find similar assets\n        similar = await self._repository.find_similar_assets(\n            workspace_id=workspace_id,\n            query_embedding=ref_embedding,\n            limit=limit * 2,  # Get more for diversity filtering\n            min_similarity=0.6,\n            exclude_asset_ids=[reference_asset_id],\n            min_quality_score=config.min_quality_score,\n        )\n\n        # Get engagement scores\n        asset_ids = [s[\"asset_id\"] for s in similar]\n        engagement_map = await self._repository.get_engagement_scores_for_assets(\n            workspace_id, asset_ids\n        )\n\n        # Score and rank\n        items = []\n        for rank, asset_data in enumerate(similar[:limit]):\n            asset_id = asset_data[\"asset_id\"]\n            engagement_data = engagement_map.get(asset_id, {})\n\n            similarity_score = asset_data.get(\"similarity_score\", 0)\n            engagement_score = engagement_data.get(\"engagement_score\", 0)\n            quality_score = asset_data.get(\"overall_quality_score\", 50)\n\n            final_score = (\n                config.weights.similarity * similarity_score +\n                config.weights.engagement * (engagement_score / 100) +\n                config.weights.quality * (quality_score / 100)\n            )\n\n            items.append({\n                \"asset_id\": asset_id,\n                \"rank_position\": rank + 1,\n                \"final_score\": final_score,\n                \"similarity_score\": similarity_score,\n                \"engagement_score\": engagement_score,\n                \"quality_score\": quality_score,\n                \"scene_category\": asset_data.get(\"scene_category\"),\n                \"visual_tags\": asset_data.get(\"scene_tags\", []),\n                \"match_reasons\": [\n                    {\n                        \"reason\": \"style_match\",\n                        \"detail\": f\"Visual similarity: {similarity_score:.0%}\",\n                        \"weight\": config.weights.similarity,\n                    },\n                ],\n                \"score_breakdown\": {\n                    \"similarity_score\": similarity_score,\n                    \"engagement_score\": engagement_score,\n                    \"quality_score\": quality_score,\n                },\n            })\n\n        return items\n\n    async def _generate_personalized(\n        self,\n        workspace_id: UUID,\n        client_id: UUID | None,\n        gallery_ids: list[UUID] | None,\n        limit: int,\n        config: RecommendationConfig,\n    ) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:\n        \"\"\"Generate personalized recommendations based on client preferences.\"\"\"\n        if not client_id:\n            return await self._generate_by_engagement(workspace_id, gallery_ids, limit, config)\n\n        # Get client preferences\n        prefs = await self._repository.get_client_preference(workspace_id, client_id)\n        if not prefs or not prefs.get(\"preference_embedding\"):\n            # Fall back to engagement-based\n            return await self._generate_by_engagement(workspace_id, gallery_ids, limit, config)\n\n        pref_embedding = prefs[\"preference_embedding\"]\n        if isinstance(pref_embedding, str):\n            pref_embedding = [float(x) for x in pref_embedding.strip(\"[]\").split(\",\")]\n\n        # Find assets similar to preference embedding\n        similar = await self._repository.find_similar_assets(\n            workspace_id=workspace_id,\n            query_embedding=pref_embedding,\n            limit=limit * 2,\n            min_similarity=0.5,\n            gallery_ids=gallery_ids,\n            min_quality_score=config.min_quality_score,\n        )\n\n        # Get engagement scores\n        asset_ids = [s[\"asset_id\"] for s in similar]\n        engagement_map = await self._repository.get_engagement_scores_for_assets(\n            workspace_id, asset_ids\n        )\n\n        # Score with personalization boost\n        items = []\n        preferred_scenes = prefs.get(\"preferred_scenes\", [])\n\n        for rank, asset_data in enumerate(similar[:limit]):\n            asset_id = asset_data[\"asset_id\"]\n            engagement_data = engagement_map.get(asset_id, {})\n\n            similarity_score = asset_data.get(\"similarity_score\", 0)\n            engagement_score = engagement_data.get(\"engagement_score\", 0)\n            quality_score = asset_data.get(\"overall_quality_score\", 50)\n\n            # Boost for matching preferred scenes\n            relevance_score = 0.5\n            scene = asset_data.get(\"scene_category\")\n            if scene and scene in preferred_scenes:\n                relevance_score = 0.9\n\n            final_score = (\n                config.weights.similarity * similarity_score +\n                config.weights.engagement * (engagement_score / 100) +\n                config.weights.quality * (quality_score / 100) +\n                config.weights.relevance * relevance_score\n            )\n\n            match_reasons = [\n                {\n                    \"reason\": \"personalized_match\",\n                    \"detail\": f\"Matches your preferences ({similarity_score:.0%})\",\n                    \"weight\": config.weights.similarity,\n                },\n            ]\n            if scene in preferred_scenes:\n                match_reasons.append({\n                    \"reason\": \"preferred_style\",\n                    \"detail\": f\"Your preferred style: {scene}\",\n                    \"weight\": config.weights.relevance,\n                })\n\n            items.append({\n                \"asset_id\": asset_id,\n                \"rank_position\": rank + 1,\n                \"final_score\": final_score,\n                \"similarity_score\": similarity_score,\n                \"engagement_score\": engagement_score,\n                \"quality_score\": quality_score,\n                \"relevance_score\": relevance_score,\n                \"scene_category\": scene,\n                \"visual_tags\": asset_data.get(\"scene_tags\", []),\n                \"match_reasons\": match_reasons,\n                \"score_breakdown\": {\n                    \"similarity_score\": similarity_score,\n                    \"engagement_score\": engagement_score,\n                    \"quality_score\": quality_score,\n                    \"relevance_score\": relevance_score,\n                },\n            })\n\n        return items\n\n    async def _generate_hero_shots(\n        self,\n        workspace_id: UUID,\n        gallery_ids: list[UUID] | None,\n        limit: int,\n        config: RecommendationConfig,\n    ) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:\n        \"\"\"Generate hero shot recommendations - best overall quality.\"\"\"\n        top_assets = await self._repository.get_top_engaged_assets(\n            workspace_id=workspace_id,\n            gallery_ids=gallery_ids,\n            limit=limit * 3,  # Get more for diversity\n            min_engagement_score=config.min_engagement_score,\n        )\n\n        # Apply diversity selection\n        items = []\n        selected_scenes = []\n\n        for asset_data in top_assets:\n            scene = asset_data.get(\"scene_category\")\n\n            # Diversity check\n            if config.diversity_factor > 0:\n                scene_count = selected_scenes.count(scene)\n                max_per_scene = max(2, limit // 4)\n                if scene_count >= max_per_scene:\n                    continue\n\n            engagement_score = asset_data.get(\"engagement_score\", 0)\n            quality_score = asset_data.get(\"overall_quality_score\", 50)\n            aesthetic_score = asset_data.get(\"aesthetic_score\", 50)\n\n            final_score = (\n                config.weights.engagement * (engagement_score / 100) +\n                config.weights.quality * (quality_score / 100) +\n                0.2 * (aesthetic_score / 100) if aesthetic_score else 0\n            )\n\n            items.append({\n                \"asset_id\": asset_data[\"asset_id\"],\n                \"rank_position\": len(items) + 1,\n                \"final_score\": final_score,\n                \"engagement_score\": engagement_score,\n                \"quality_score\": quality_score,\n                \"scene_category\": scene,\n                \"match_reasons\": [\n                    {\n                        \"reason\": \"high_engagement\",\n                        \"detail\": f\"Top {asset_data.get('score_percentile', 0):.0%} engagement\",\n                        \"weight\": config.weights.engagement,\n                    },\n                    {\n                        \"reason\": \"quality_shot\",\n                        \"detail\": f\"Quality score: {quality_score:.0f}/100\",\n                        \"weight\": config.weights.quality,\n                    },\n                ],\n                \"score_breakdown\": {\n                    \"engagement_score\": engagement_score,\n                    \"quality_score\": quality_score,\n                },\n            })\n\n            selected_scenes.append(scene)\n\n            if len(items) >= limit:\n                break\n\n        return items\n\n    async def _generate_trending(\n        self,\n        workspace_id: UUID,\n        gallery_ids: list[UUID] | None,\n        limit: int,\n        config: RecommendationConfig,\n    ) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:\n        \"\"\"Generate trending recommendations - recent high performers.\"\"\"\n        # Get assets with recent high engagement\n        top_assets = await self._repository.get_top_engaged_assets(\n            workspace_id=workspace_id,\n            gallery_ids=gallery_ids,\n            limit=limit * 2,\n            min_engagement_score=30,  # Lower threshold, focus on recency\n        )\n\n        # Sort by recent activity (views_last_7d, favorites_last_7d)\n        sorted_assets = sorted(\n            top_assets,\n            key=lambda x: (x.get(\"views_last_7d\", 0) + x.get(\"favorites_last_7d\", 0) * 3),\n            reverse=True,\n        )\n\n        items = []\n        for rank, asset_data in enumerate(sorted_assets[:limit]):\n            engagement_score = asset_data.get(\"engagement_score\", 0)\n            views_7d = asset_data.get(\"views_last_7d\", 0)\n            favorites_7d = asset_data.get(\"favorites_last_7d\", 0)\n\n            recency_score = min(1.0, (views_7d + favorites_7d * 3) / 50)\n\n            final_score = (\n                config.weights.engagement * (engagement_score / 100) +\n                config.weights.recency * recency_score\n            )\n\n            items.append({\n                \"asset_id\": asset_data[\"asset_id\"],\n                \"rank_position\": rank + 1,\n                \"final_score\": final_score,\n                \"engagement_score\": engagement_score,\n                \"recency_score\": recency_score,\n                \"scene_category\": asset_data.get(\"scene_category\"),\n                \"match_reasons\": [\n                    {\n                        \"reason\": \"trending\",\n                        \"detail\": f\"{views_7d} views, {favorites_7d} favorites this week\",\n                        \"weight\": config.weights.recency,\n                    },\n                ],\n                \"score_breakdown\": {\n                    \"engagement_score\": engagement_score,\n                    \"recency_score\": recency_score,\n                },\n            })\n\n        return items\n\n    async def _generate_seasonal(\n        self,\n        workspace_id: UUID,\n        target_season: str | None,\n        gallery_ids: list[UUID] | None,\n        limit: int,\n        config: RecommendationConfig,\n    ) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:\n        \"\"\"Generate seasonal recommendations.\"\"\"\n        if not target_season:\n            # Default to current season\n            month = datetime.now().month\n            if month in [3, 4, 5]:\n                target_season = \"spring\"\n            elif month in [6, 7, 8]:\n                target_season = \"summer\"\n            elif month in [9, 10, 11]:\n                target_season = \"fall\"\n            else:\n                target_season = \"winter\"\n\n        season_attrs = SEASON_ATTRIBUTES.get(target_season, {})\n        preferred_colors = season_attrs.get(\"colors\", [])\n        preferred_brightness = season_attrs.get(\"brightness\")\n\n        # Get top assets and filter by seasonal attributes\n        top_assets = await self._repository.get_top_engaged_assets(\n            workspace_id=workspace_id,\n            gallery_ids=gallery_ids,\n            limit=limit * 3,\n            min_engagement_score=config.min_engagement_score,\n        )\n\n        items = []\n        for asset_data in top_assets:\n            color_palette = asset_data.get(\"color_palette\")\n            brightness = asset_data.get(\"brightness_level\")\n\n            # Calculate seasonal relevance\n            relevance = 0.5\n            if color_palette and color_palette in preferred_colors:\n                relevance += 0.3\n            if brightness and brightness == preferred_brightness:\n                relevance += 0.2\n\n            engagement_score = asset_data.get(\"engagement_score\", 0)\n            quality_score = asset_data.get(\"overall_quality_score\", 50)\n\n            final_score = (\n                config.weights.engagement * (engagement_score / 100) +\n                config.weights.quality * (quality_score / 100) +\n                config.weights.relevance * relevance\n            )\n\n            items.append({\n                \"asset_id\": asset_data[\"asset_id\"],\n                \"rank_position\": len(items) + 1,\n                \"final_score\": final_score,\n                \"engagement_score\": engagement_score,\n                \"quality_score\": quality_score,\n                \"relevance_score\": relevance,\n                \"scene_category\": asset_data.get(\"scene_category\"),\n                \"match_reasons\": [\n                    {\n                        \"reason\": \"seasonal_match\",\n                        \"detail\": f\"Perfect for {target_season}\",\n                        \"weight\": config.weights.relevance,\n                    },\n                ],\n                \"score_breakdown\": {\n                    \"engagement_score\": engagement_score,\n                    \"quality_score\": quality_score,\n                    \"relevance_score\": relevance,\n                },\n            })\n\n            if len(items) >= limit:\n                break\n\n        return items\n\n    async def _generate_portfolio_selection(\n        self,\n        workspace_id: UUID,\n        client_id: UUID | None,\n        gallery_id: UUID | None,\n        occasion_type: str | None,\n        limit: int,\n        config: RecommendationConfig,\n    ) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:\n        \"\"\"Generate optimized portfolio selection for a client.\"\"\"\n        gallery_ids = [gallery_id] if gallery_id else None\n\n        # If client has preferences, use personalized\n        if client_id:\n            prefs = await self._repository.get_client_preference(workspace_id, client_id)\n            if prefs and prefs.get(\"preference_embedding\"):\n                return await self._generate_personalized(\n                    workspace_id, client_id, gallery_ids, limit, config\n                )\n\n        # Otherwise, use occasion-based selection\n        relevant_scenes = None\n        if occasion_type and occasion_type in OCCASION_SCENE_MAPPING:\n            relevant_scenes = OCCASION_SCENE_MAPPING[occasion_type]\n\n        # Get top assets\n        all_items = []\n\n        if relevant_scenes:\n            # Get assets from each relevant scene category\n            items_per_scene = limit // len(relevant_scenes) + 1\n            for scene in relevant_scenes:\n                scene_assets = await self._repository.get_top_engaged_assets(\n                    workspace_id=workspace_id,\n                    gallery_ids=gallery_ids,\n                    limit=items_per_scene,\n                    scene_category=scene.value,\n                    min_engagement_score=config.min_engagement_score,\n                )\n                all_items.extend(scene_assets)\n        else:\n            all_items = await self._repository.get_top_engaged_assets(\n                workspace_id=workspace_id,\n                gallery_ids=gallery_ids,\n                limit=limit * 2,\n                min_engagement_score=config.min_engagement_score,\n            )\n\n        # Score and dedupe\n        seen_ids = set()\n        items = []\n\n        for asset_data in all_items:\n            asset_id = asset_data[\"asset_id\"]\n            if asset_id in seen_ids:\n                continue\n            seen_ids.add(asset_id)\n\n            engagement_score = asset_data.get(\"engagement_score\", 0)\n            quality_score = asset_data.get(\"overall_quality_score\", 50)\n\n            final_score = (\n                config.weights.engagement * (engagement_score / 100) +\n                config.weights.quality * (quality_score / 100)\n            )\n\n            items.append({\n                \"asset_id\": asset_id,\n                \"rank_position\": len(items) + 1,\n                \"final_score\": final_score,\n                \"engagement_score\": engagement_score,\n                \"quality_score\": quality_score,\n                \"scene_category\": asset_data.get(\"scene_category\"),\n                \"match_reasons\": [\n                    {\n                        \"reason\": \"curated_selection\",\n                        \"detail\": \"Optimized for your portfolio\",\n                        \"weight\": 1.0,\n                    },\n                ],\n                \"score_breakdown\": {\n                    \"engagement_score\": engagement_score,\n                    \"quality_score\": quality_score,\n                },\n            })\n\n            if len(items) >= limit:\n                break\n\n        # Sort by score\n        items.sort(key=lambda x: x[\"final_score\"], reverse=True)\n        for i, item in enumerate(items):\n            item[\"rank_position\"] = i + 1\n\n        return items\n\n    async def _generate_print_worthy(\n        self,\n        workspace_id: UUID,\n        gallery_ids: list[UUID] | None,\n        limit: int,\n        config: RecommendationConfig,\n    ) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:\n        \"\"\"Generate print-worthy recommendations - high technical quality.\"\"\"\n        # Focus on quality scores\n        modified_config = RecommendationConfig(\n            weights=ScoringWeights(\n                quality=0.50,\n                engagement=0.25,\n                similarity=0.05,\n                relevance=0.10,\n                recency=0.05,\n                diversity=0.05,\n            ).normalize(),\n            min_quality_score=70,  # Higher quality threshold\n        )\n\n        return await self._generate_hero_shots(\n            workspace_id, gallery_ids, limit, modified_config\n        )\n\n    async def _generate_social_media(\n        self,\n        workspace_id: UUID,\n        gallery_ids: list[UUID] | None,\n        limit: int,\n        config: RecommendationConfig,\n    ) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:\n        \"\"\"Generate social media optimized recommendations.\"\"\"\n        # Focus on engagement and aesthetics\n        modified_config = RecommendationConfig(\n            weights=ScoringWeights(\n                engagement=0.40,\n                quality=0.20,\n                similarity=0.10,\n                relevance=0.10,\n                recency=0.15,\n                diversity=0.05,\n            ).normalize(),\n        )\n\n        return await self._generate_trending(\n            workspace_id, gallery_ids, limit, modified_config\n        )\n\n    async def _generate_album_cover(\n        self,\n        workspace_id: UUID,\n        gallery_id: UUID | None,\n        limit: int,\n        config: RecommendationConfig,\n    ) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:\n        \"\"\"Generate album cover candidates.\"\"\"\n        gallery_ids = [gallery_id] if gallery_id else None\n\n        # Hero shots make good covers\n        return await self._generate_hero_shots(\n            workspace_id, gallery_ids, limit, config\n        )\n\n    async def _generate_by_engagement(\n        self,\n        workspace_id: UUID,\n        gallery_ids: list[UUID] | None,\n        limit: int,\n        config: RecommendationConfig,\n    ) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:\n        \"\"\"Default: generate by engagement score.\"\"\"\n        return await self._generate_hero_shots(\n            workspace_id, gallery_ids, limit, config\n        )\n\n    # =========================================================================\n    # A/B Testing\n    # =========================================================================\n\n    async def _get_active_ab_test(\n        self,\n        workspace_id: UUID,\n        recommendation_type: RecommendationType,\n        client_id: UUID | None,\n    ) -> dict[str, Any] | None:\n        \"\"\"Get active A/B test for this recommendation context.\"\"\"\n        client_type = None\n        if client_id:\n            prefs = await self._repository.get_client_preference(workspace_id, client_id)\n            client_type = prefs.get(\"client_type\") if prefs else None\n\n        return await self._repository.get_active_ab_test(\n            workspace_id=workspace_id,\n            recommendation_type=recommendation_type.value,\n            client_type=client_type,\n        )\n\n    def _assign_ab_variant(\n        self,\n        ab_test: dict[str, Any],\n        workspace_id: UUID,\n        client_id: UUID | None,\n    ) -> str:\n        \"\"\"Assign a variant for an A/B test.\"\"\"\n        # Create deterministic hash for consistent assignment\n        hash_input = f\"{ab_test['test_id']}:{workspace_id}\"\n        if client_id:\n            hash_input += f\":{client_id}\"\n\n        hash_value = int(hashlib.md5(hash_input.encode()).hexdigest(), 16)\n\n        # Get variants and their weights\n        variants = ab_test.get(\"variants\", [])\n        if not variants:\n            return \"control\"\n\n        total_weight = sum(v.get(\"weight\", 50) for v in variants) + 50  # +50 for control\n        roll = hash_value % total_weight\n\n        # Check control first\n        if roll < 50:\n            return \"control\"\n\n        # Check variants\n        cumulative = 50\n        for variant in variants:\n            weight = variant.get(\"weight\", 50)\n            if roll < cumulative + weight:\n                return variant[\"name\"]\n            cumulative += weight\n\n        return \"control\"\n\n    def _apply_ab_variant_config(\n        self,\n        config: RecommendationConfig,\n        ab_test: dict[str, Any],\n        variant_name: str,\n    ) -> RecommendationConfig:\n        \"\"\"Apply variant configuration to recommendation config.\"\"\"\n        if variant_name == \"control\":\n            variant_config = ab_test.get(\"control_config\", {})\n        else:\n            variants = ab_test.get(\"variants\", [])\n            variant_config = {}\n            for v in variants:\n                if v[\"name\"] == variant_name:\n                    variant_config = v.get(\"config\", {})\n                    break\n\n        # Apply config overrides\n        if \"weights\" in variant_config:\n            config.weights = ScoringWeights(**variant_config[\"weights\"]).normalize()\n        if \"diversity_factor\" in variant_config:\n            config.diversity_factor = variant_config[\"diversity_factor\"]\n        if \"min_quality_score\" in variant_config:\n            config.min_quality_score = variant_config[\"min_quality_score\"]\n\n        return config\n\n    async def create_ab_test(\n        self,\n        workspace_id: UUID,\n        test_name: str,\n        test_type: str,\n        control_config: dict,\n        variants: list[dict],\n        start_date: datetime,\n        **kwargs,\n    ) -> dict[str, Any]:\n        \"\"\"Create a new A/B test.\"\"\"\n        return await self._repository.create_ab_test(\n            workspace_id=workspace_id,\n            test_name=test_name,\n            test_type=test_type,\n            control_config=control_config,\n            variants=variants,\n            start_date=start_date,\n            **kwargs,\n        )\n\n    async def start_ab_test(\n        self,\n        workspace_id: UUID,\n        test_id: UUID,\n    ) -> None:\n        \"\"\"Start an A/B test.\"\"\"\n        await self._repository.update_ab_test_status(\n            workspace_id, test_id, \"active\"\n        )\n        logger.info(f\"Started A/B test {test_id}\")\n\n    async def conclude_ab_test(\n        self,\n        workspace_id: UUID,\n        test_id: UUID,\n        winner_variant: str | None = None,\n    ) -> None:\n        \"\"\"Conclude an A/B test.\"\"\"\n        await self._repository.update_ab_test_status(\n            workspace_id=workspace_id,\n            test_id=test_id,\n            status=\"concluded\",\n            winner_variant=winner_variant,\n        )\n        logger.info(f\"Concluded A/B test {test_id} with winner: {winner_variant}\")\n\n    async def get_ab_test_results(\n        self,\n        workspace_id: UUID,\n        test_id: UUID,\n    ) -> dict[str, Any]:\n        \"\"\"Get A/B test results with statistical analysis.\"\"\"\n        test = await self._repository.get_ab_test(workspace_id, test_id)\n        if not test:\n            raise ValueError(f\"A/B test {test_id} not found\")\n\n        # Get recommendations assigned to this test\n        recommendations = await self._repository.list_recommendations(\n            workspace_id=workspace_id,\n            limit=10000,\n        )\n\n        test_recommendations = [\n            r for r in recommendations\n            if r.get(\"ab_test_id\") == test_id\n        ]\n\n        # Calculate metrics per variant\n        variant_results = {}\n        variants = [\"control\"] + [v[\"name\"] for v in test.get(\"variants\", [])]\n\n        for variant in variants:\n            variant_recs = [r for r in test_recommendations if r.get(\"ab_variant\") == variant]\n\n            total = len(variant_recs)\n            if total == 0:\n                variant_results[variant] = {\n                    \"sample_size\": 0,\n                    \"conversion_rate\": 0,\n                    \"avg_selection_rate\": 0,\n                }\n                continue\n\n            # Calculate metrics\n            items_selected = sum(r.get(\"items_selected\", 0) for r in variant_recs)\n            items_recommended = sum(r.get(\"total_recommended\", 0) for r in variant_recs)\n\n            selection_rate = items_selected / items_recommended if items_recommended > 0 else 0\n            avg_rating = sum(r.get(\"user_rating\", 0) for r in variant_recs if r.get(\"user_rating\")) / total\n\n            variant_results[variant] = {\n                \"sample_size\": total,\n                \"items_recommended\": items_recommended,\n                \"items_selected\": items_selected,\n                \"selection_rate\": selection_rate,\n                \"avg_rating\": avg_rating,\n            }\n\n        # Statistical comparison\n        control = variant_results.get(\"control\", {})\n        significance_results = {}\n\n        for variant_name, results in variant_results.items():\n            if variant_name == \"control\":\n                continue\n\n            # Simple z-test for proportions\n            control_rate = control.get(\"selection_rate\", 0)\n            variant_rate = results.get(\"selection_rate\", 0)\n            control_n = control.get(\"sample_size\", 0)\n            variant_n = results.get(\"sample_size\", 0)\n\n            if control_n > 0 and variant_n > 0 and control_rate > 0:\n                lift = (variant_rate - control_rate) / control_rate\n                # Simplified p-value calculation\n                pooled_rate = (control_rate * control_n + variant_rate * variant_n) / (control_n + variant_n)\n                se = (pooled_rate * (1 - pooled_rate) * (1/control_n + 1/variant_n)) ** 0.5\n                z = (variant_rate - control_rate) / se if se > 0 else 0\n                # Approximate p-value\n                p_value = 2 * (1 - min(0.9999, abs(z) / 4))  # Simplified\n\n                significance_results[variant_name] = {\n                    \"lift\": lift,\n variant_results,\n            \"significance_results\": significance_results,\n            \"total_samples\": sum(v.get(\"sample_size\", 0) for v in variant_results.values()),\n {feedback_type.value} feedback for recommendation {recommendation_id}\"\n        )\n\n        return feedback\n\n    async def record_item_interaction(\n        self,\n        workspace_id: UUID,\n min_quality_score,\n                \"diversity_factor\": diversity_factor,\n            },\n        )\n\n        return _format_recommendation_response(result)\n\n    except Exception as e:\n        raise HTTPException(status_code=500, detail=f\"Hero shots generation failed: {e}\")\n\n\n@router.post(\n    \"/seasonal\",\n    response_model=RecommendationResponse,\n    summary=\"Get seasonal le=100),\n    occasion_type: str | None = Query(default=None),\n    current_user: dict = Depends(get_current_user),\n    service: PortfolioRecommendationService = Depends(get_portfolio_recommendation_service),\n):\n    \"\"\"Get seasonal recommendations.\"\"\"\n    if target_season not in [\"spring\", \"summer\", \"fall\", \"winter\"]:\n        raise Valid values: spring, summer, fall, winter\",\n        )\n\n    try:\n        result = await service.generate_recommendations(\n            workspace_id=workspace_id,\n limit=limit,\n        )\n\n        return _format_recommendation_response(result)\n\n    except Exception as e:\n        raise HTTPException(status_code=500, detail=f\"Seasonal recommendations failed: {e}\")\n\n\n@router.get(\n    \"/{recommendation_id}\",\n    response_model=RecommendationResponse,\n    summary=\"Get recommendation details\",\n    description=\"Get details of a specific Query(default=None),\n    gallery_id: UUID | None = Query(default=None),\n    limit: int = Query(default=50, ge=1, le=200),\n    offset: int = Query(default=0, ge=0),\n    current_user: dict = Valid types: {[t.value for t in RecommendationType]}\",\n            )\n\n    if status:\n        try:\n            RecommendationStatus(status)\n            filters[\"status\"] = status\n        except ValueError:\n            raise HTTPException(\n                status_code=400,\n                detail=f\"Invalid status. gallery_id\n\n    results = await service.list_recommendations(workspace_id, **filters)\n\n    return [\n        {\n            \"recommendation_id\": str(r[\"recommendation_id\"]),\n prefs.get(\"preferred_styles\", []),\n        \"preferred_colors\": prefs.get(\"preferred_colors\", []),\n        \"preferred_compositions\": prefs.get(\"preferred_compositions\", []),\n prefs.get(\"embedding_sample_size\", 0),\n        \"total_galleries_viewed\": prefs.get(\"total_galleries_viewed\", 0),\n        \"total_selections_made\": prefs.get(\"total_selections_made\", 0),\n        \"preference_confidence\": prefs.get(\"preference_confidence\", 0),\n        \"last_interaction_at\": prefs[\"last_interaction_at\"].isoformat() if prefs.get(\"last_interaction_at\") else None,\n }\n\n\n@router.put(\n    \"/clients/{client_id}/preferences\",\n    response_model=ClientPreferenceResponse,\n    summary=\"Update client preferences\",\n    description=\"Manually update client result[\"last_interaction_at\"].isoformat() if result.get(\"last_interaction_at\") else None,\n        \"created_at\": result[\"created_at\"].isoformat() if result.get(\"created_at\") else None,\n        \"updated_at\": result[\"updated_at\"].isoformat() if result.get(\"updated_at\") else None,\n    }\n\n\n@router.post(\n    \"/clients/{client_id}/learn\",\n result.get(\"updated_at\") else None,\n    }\n\n\n# =========================================================================\n# A/B Testing Endpoints\n# =========================================================================\n\n\n@router.post(\n    \"/ab-tests\",\n    response_model=ABTestResponse,\n    summary=\"Create A/B test\",\n    description=\"Create a new A/B test for recommendation algorithms.\",\n)\nasync def create_ab_test(\n    request: CreateABTestRequest,\n    workspace_id: UUID = Path(..., description=\"Workspace ID\"),\n    current_user: dict = Depends(get_current_user),\n    service: PortfolioRecommendationService = Depends(get_portfolio_recommendation_service),\n):\n    \"\"\"Create a new A/B test.\"\"\"\n    await require_permissions(current_user, workspace_id, [\"manage_recommendations\"])\n\n    try:\n        test_type = ABTestType(request.test_type)\n    except ValueError:\n        raise HTTPException(\n            status_code=400,\n            detail=f\"Invalid test type. Valid types: {[t.value for t in ABTestType]}\",\n        )\n\n    try:\n        primary_metric = PrimaryMetric(request.primary_metric)\n    except ValueError:\n        raise HTTPException(\n            status_code=400,\n            detail=f\"Invalid primary metric. Valid metrics: {[m.value for m in PrimaryMetric]}\",\n        )\n\n    # Validate recommendation types\n    if request.target_recommendation_types:\n        for rt in request.target_recommendation_types:\n            try:\n                RecommendationType(rt)\n            except ValueError:\n                raise HTTPException(\n                    status_code=400,\n                    detail=f\"Invalid recommendation type: {rt}\",\n                )\n\n    # Validate client types\n    if request.target_client_types:\n        for ct in request.target_client_types:\n            try:\n                ClientType(ct)\n            except ValueError:\n                raise HTTPException(\n                    status_code=400,\n                    detail=f\"Invalid client type: {ct}\",\n                )\n\n    test = await service.create_ab_test(\n        workspace_id=workspace_id,\n        test_name=request.test_name,\n        test_type=test_type.value,\n        control_config=request.control_config,\n        variants=request.variants,\n        start_date=request.start_date,\n        test_description=request.test_description,\n        hypothesis=request.hypothesis,\n        target_recommendation_types=request.target_recommendation_types,\n        target_client_types=request.target_client_types,\n        traffic_percentage=request.traffic_percentage,\n        end_date=request.end_date,\n        min_sample_size=request.min_sample_size,\n        significance_level=request.significance_level,\n        minimum_detectable_effect=request.minimum_detectable_effect,\n        primary_metric=primary_metric.value,\n        secondary_metrics=request.secondary_metrics,\n    )\n\n    return _format_ab_test_response(test)\n\n\n@router.get(\n    \"/ab-tests\",\n    response_model=list[ABTestResponse],\n    summary=\"List A/B Query(default=None),\n    limit: int = Query(default=50, ge=1, le=200),\n    offset: int = Query(default=0, ge=0),\n    current_user: dict = Depends(get_current_user),\n    service: PortfolioRecommendationService = Depends(get_portfolio_recommendation_service),\n):\n    \"\"\"List A/B tests.\"\"\"\n    if status:\n        try:\n            ABTestStatus(status)\n        except ValueError:\n            raise HTTPException(\n                status_code=400,\n                detail=f\"Invalid status. Valid values: {[s.value for s in ABTestStatus]}\",\n            )\n\n    tests = await service._repository.list_ab_tests(\n        workspace_id, status=status, limit=limit, offset=offset\n    )\n\n    return [_format_ab_test_response(t) for t in tests]\n\n\n@router.get(\n    \"/ab-tests/{test_id}\",\n    response_model=ABTestResponse,\n    summary=\"Get A/B test details\",\n    description=\"Get details of an A/B test.\",\n)\nasync def get_ab_test(\n    workspace_id: UUID = Path(..., description=\"Workspace ID\"),\n    test_id: UUID = Path(..., description=\"Test ID\"),\n    current_user: dict = Depends(get_current_user),\n    service: PortfolioRecommendationService = Depends(get_portfolio_recommendation_service),\n):\n    \"\"\"Get A/B test details.\"\"\"\n    test = await service._repository.get_ab_test(workspace_id, test_id)\n    if not test:\n        raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail=\"A/B test not found\")\n\n    return _format_ab_test_response(test)\n\n\n@router.post(\n    \"/ab-tests/{test_id}/start\",\n    response_model=ABTestResponse,\n    summary=\"Start A/B test\",\n    description=\"Activate a draft A/B Depends(get_current_user),\n    service: PortfolioRecommendationService = Depends(get_portfolio_recommendation_service),\n):\n    \"\"\"Start an A/B test.\"\"\"\n    await require_permissions(current_user, workspace_id, [\"manage_recommendations\"])\n\n    await service.start_ab_test(workspace_id, test_id)\n\n    test = await service._repository.get_ab_test(workspace_id, test_id)\n    if not test:\n        raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail=\"A/B test not found\")\n\n    return _format_ab_test_response(test)\n\n\n@router.post(\n    \"/ab-tests/{test_id}/conclude\",\n    response_model=ABTestResponse,\n    summary=\"Conclude A/B test\",\n    description=\"Conclude an A/B test with optional winner selection.\",\n)\nasync def conclude_ab_test(\n    workspace_id: UUID = Path(..., description=\"Workspace ID\"),\n    test_id: UUID = Path(..., description=\"Test ID\"),\n    winner_variant: str | None = Query(default=None),\n    current_user: dict = Depends(get_current_user),\n    service: PortfolioRecommendationService = Depends(get_portfolio_recommendation_service),\n):\n    \"\"\"Conclude an A/B test.\"\"\"\n    await require_permissions(current_user, workspace_id, [\"manage_recommendations\"])\n\n    await service.conclude_ab_test(workspace_id, test_id, winner_variant)\n\n    test = await service._repository.get_ab_test(workspace_id, test_id)\n    if not test:\n        raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail=\"A/B test not found\")\n\n    return _format_ab_test_response(test)\n\n\n@router.get(\n    \"/ab-tests/{test_id}/results\",\n    response_model=ABTestResultsResponse,\n    summary=\"Get A/B test results\",\n    description=\"Get statistical analysis of A/B test results.\",\n)\nasync def get_ab_test_results(\n    workspace_id: UUID = Path(..., description=\"Workspace ID\"),\n    test_id: UUID = Path(..., description=\"Test ID\"),\n    current_user: dict = Depends(get_current_user),\n    service: PortfolioRecommendationService = Depends(get_portfolio_recommendation_service),\n):\n    \"\"\"Get A/B test results with statistical analysis.\"\"\"\n    try:\n        results = await =========================================================================\n# Feedback Endpoints\n# =========================================================================\n\n\n@router.post(\n    \"/{recommendation_id}/feedback\",\n    response_model=FeedbackResponse,\n    summary=\"Submit recommendation feedback\",\n    description=\"Submit feedback on a recommendation.\",\n)\nasync def submit_feedback(\n    request: FeedbackRequest,\n    workspace_id: UUID = Path(..., description=\"Workspace ID\"),\n    recommendation_id: UUID = Path(..., description=\"Recommendation ID\"),\n    current_user: dict = Depends(get_current_user),\n    service: PortfolioRecommendationService = Depends(get_portfolio_recommendation_service),\n):\n    \"\"\"Submit feedback on a recommendation.\"\"\"\n    try:\n        feedback_type = FeedbackType(request.feedback_type)\n    except ValueError:\n        raise HTTPException(\n            status_code=400,\n            detail=f\"Invalid feedback type. Valid types: {[t.value for t in FeedbackType]}\",\n        )\n\n    feedback = await service.record_feedback(\n        workspace_id=workspace_id,\n        recommendation_id=recommendation_id,\n        feedback_type=feedback_type,\n        feedback_source=FeedbackSource.USER,\n        user_id=UUID(current_user[\"user_id\"]) if current_user.get(\"user_id\") else None,\n        rating=request.rating,\n        comment=request.comment,\n        selected_count=request.selected_count,\n        rejected_count=request.rejected_count,\n    )\n\n    return {\n        \"feedback_id\": str(feedback[\"feedback_id\"]),\n        \"recommendation_id\": str(feedback[\"recommendation_id\"]),\n        \"feedback_source\": feedback[\"feedback_source\"],\n        \"feedback_type\": feedback[\"feedback_type\"],\n        \"rating\": feedback.get(\"rating\"),\n        \"comment\": feedback.get(\"comment\"),\n        \"created_at\": feedback[\"created_at\"].isoformat() if feedback.get(\"created_at\") else None,\n    }\n\n\n@router.post(\n    \"/items/{item_id}/interaction\",\n    summary=\"Record item interaction\",\n    description=\"Record an interaction with a recommendation item.\",\n)\nasync def record_item_interaction(\n    request: ItemInteractionRequest,\n    workspace_id: UUID = Path(..., description=\"Workspace ID\"),\n    item_id: UUID = Path(..., description=\"Item ID\"),\n    current_user: dict = Depends(get_current_user),\n    service: PortfolioRecommendationService = Depends(get_portfolio_recommendation_service),\n):\n    \"\"\"Record interaction with a recommendation item.\"\"\"\n    valid_types = [\"view\", \"select\", \"download\", \"reject\"]\n    if request.interaction_type not in valid_types:\n        raise HTTPException(\n            status_code=400,\n            detail=f\"Invalid interaction type. Valid types: {valid_types}\",\n        )\n\n    await service.record_item_interaction(\n        workspace_id=workspace_id,\n        item_id=item_id,\n        interaction_type=request.interaction_type,\n        view_duration_ms=request.view_duration_ms,\n    )\n\n    return {\"status\": \"recorded\", \"item_id\": str(item_id)}\n\n\n# =========================================================================\n# Top Assets & Analytics Endpoints\n# =========================================================================\n\n\n@router.get(\n    \"/top-assets\",\n    response_model=list[TopAssetResponse],\n    summary=\"Get top performing assets\",\n    description=\"Get top performing assets by engagement score.\",\n)\nasync def get_top_assets(\n    workspace_id: UUID = Path(..., description=\"Workspace ID\"),\n    limit: int = Query(default=50, ge=1, le=200),\n    scene_category: str | None = Query(default=None),\n    current_user: dict = Depends(get_current_user),\n    service: PortfolioRecommendationService = Depends(get_portfolio_recommendation_service),\n):\n    \"\"\"Get top performing assets.\"\"\"\n    if scene_category:\n        try:\n            SceneCategory(scene_category)\n        except ValueError:\n            raise HTTPException(\n                status_code=400,\n                detail=f\"Invalid scene category. Valid categories: {[c.value for c in SceneCategory]}\",\n            )\n\n    assets = await service.get_top_assets(\n        workspace_id, limit=limit, scene_category=scene_category\n    )\n\n    return [\n        {\n            \"asset_id\": str(a[\"asset_id\"]),\n            \"gallery_id\": str(a[\"gallery_id\"]) if a.get(\"gallery_id\") else None,\n            \"engagement_score\": a.get(\"engagement_score\", 0),\n            \"total_views\": a.get(\"total_views\", 0),\n            \"total_favorites\": a.get(\"total_favorites\", 0),\n            \"total_selections\": a.get(\"total_selections\", 0),\n            \"total_downloads\": a.get(\"total_downloads\", 0),\n            \"conversion_rate\": a.get(\"conversion_rate\", 0),\n            \"scene_category\": a.get(\"scene_category\"),\n            \"aesthetic_score\": a.get(\"aesthetic_score\"),\n            \"quality_score\": a.get(\"overall_quality_score\"),\n            \"rank_in_workspace\": a.get(\"rank_in_workspace\", 0),\n            \"rank_in_category\": a.get(\"rank_in_category\"),\n            \"percentile\": a.get(\"score_percentile\", 0),\n            \"asset_url\": a.get(\"asset_url\"),\n            \"thumbnail_url\": a.get(\"thumbnail_url\"),\n        }\n        for a in assets\n    ]\n\n\n# \"\"\"Generate embeddings for multiple assets.\"\"\"\n    await require_permissions(current_user, workspace_id, [\"manage_recommendations\"])\n\n    result = await service.batch_generate_embeddings(\n }\n\n\n@router.post(\n    \"/engagement/refresh\",\n    response_model=RefreshEngagementResponse,\n    summary=\"Refresh engagement scores\",\n    description=\"Refresh engagement scores for all \"gallery_id\": str(result[\"gallery_id\"]) if result.get(\"gallery_id\") else None,\n        \"occasion_type\": result.get(\"occasion_type\"),\n        \"target_season\": },\n\n  /**\n   * Get A/B test by ID\n   */\n  async getABTest(\n    workspaceId: string,\n    testId: string\n  ): Promise<ABTest> {\n    const response = await apiClient.get<ABTest>(\n      buildPath(workspaceId, 'ab-tests', testId)\n    );\n    return response.data;\n  },\n\n  /**\n   * Start A/B test\n   */\n  async startABTest(\n    workspaceId: string,\n    testId: string\n  ): Promise<ABTest> {\n    const response = await apiClient.post<ABTest>(\n      buildPath(workspaceId, 'ab-tests', testId, 'start')\n    );\n    return response.data;\n  },\n\n  /**\n   * Conclude A/B test\n   */\n  async concludeABTest(\n    workspaceId: string,\n    testId: string,\n    winnerVariant?: string\n  ): Promise<ABTest> {\n    const params = winnerVariant ? `?winner_variant=${winnerVariant}` : '';\n    const response = await apiClient.post<ABTest>(\n      `${buildPath(workspaceId, 'ab-tests', testId, 'conclude')}${params}`\n    );\n    return response.data;\n  },\n\n  /**\n   * Get A/B test results\n   */\n  async getABTestResults(\n    workspaceId: string,\n    testId: string\n  ): Promise<ABTestResults> {\n    const response = await apiClient.get<ABTestResults>(\n      buildPath(workspaceId, 'ab-tests', testId, 'results')\n    );\n    return response.data;\n  },\n\n  // ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n  // Feedback\n  // ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\n  /**\n   * Submit feedback on a recommendation\n   */\n  async submitFeedback(\n    workspaceId: string,\n    recommendationId: string,\n    feedback: FeedbackRequest\n  ): Promise<Feedback> {\n    const response = await apiClient.post<Feedback>(\n      buildPath(workspaceId, recommendationId, 'feedback'),\n      feedback\n    );\n    return response.data;\n  },\n\n  /**\n   * Record item interaction\n   */\n  async recordItemInteraction(\n    workspaceId: string,\n    itemId: string,\n    interactionType: 'view' | 'select' | 'download' | 'reject',\n    viewDurationMs?: number\n  ): Promise<void> {\n    await apiClient.post(\n      buildPath(workspaceId, 'items', itemId, 'interaction'),\n      {\n        interaction_type: interactionType,\n        view_duration_ms: viewDurationMs,\n      }\n    );\n  },\n\n  // ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n  // Top Assets & Analytics\n  // ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\n  /**\n   * Get top performing assets\n   */\n  async getTopAssets(\n    workspaceId: string,\n    options?: {\n      limit?: number;\n      sceneCategory?: SceneCategory;\n    }\n  ): Promise<TopAsset[]> {\n    const params = new URLSearchParams();\n    if (options?.limit) params.append('limit', String(options.limit));\n    if (options?.sceneCategory) params.append('scene_category', options.sceneCategory);\n\n    const response = await apiClient.get<TopAsset[]>(\n      `${buildPath(workspaceId, 'top-assets')}?${params}`\n    );\n    return response.data;\n  },\n\n  // ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n  // Embedding Management\n  // ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\n  /**\n   * Generate batch embeddings\n   */\n  async generateBatchEmbeddings(\n    workspaceId: string,\n    request: BatchEmbeddingRequest\n  ): Promise<BatchEmbeddingResponse> {\n    const response = await apiClient.post<BatchEmbeddingResponse>(\n      buildPath(workspaceId, 'embeddings', 'batch'),\n      request\n    );\n    return response.data;\n  },\n\n  /**\n   * Refresh engagement scores\n   */\n  async refreshEngagementScores(\n    workspaceId: string\n  ): Promise<RefreshEngagementResponse> {\n    const response = await apiClient.post<RefreshEngagementResponse>(\n      buildPath(workspaceId, 'engagement', 'refresh')\n    );\n    return response.data;\n  },\n};\n\nexport default portfolioRecommendationService;\n"
}

onError: (error) => {\n        addToast({\n          variant: 'error',\n          message: error instanceof Error ? error.message : 'Failed to refresh scores',\n        });\n      },\n    });\n  }, [refreshScores, refetchTopAssets, addToast]);\n\n  return (\n    <div className=\"space-y-6\">\n      {/* Header */}\n      <div className=\"flex items-center justify-between\">\n        <div>\n          <h2 className=\"text-lg font-semibold text-text-primary flex items-center gap-2\">\n            <Sparkles size={20} className=\"text-primary\" />\n            Portfolio Recommendations\n          </h2>\n          <p className=\"text-sm text-text-tertiary\">\n            AI-powered insights to optimize your photo selections\n          </p>\n        </div>\n        <div className=\"flex gap-2\">\n          <AppButton\n            variant=\"secondary\"\n            size=\"sm\"\n            leftIcon={<RefreshCw size={16} className={isRefreshing ? 'animate-spin' : ''} />}\n            onClick={handleRefresh}\n            disabled={isRefreshing}\n          >\n            Refresh\n          </AppButton>\n          {onGenerateClick && (\n            <AppButton\n              variant=\"primary\"\n              size=\"sm\"\n              leftIcon={<Sparkles size={16} />}\n              onClick={onGenerateClick}\n            >\n              Generate\n            </AppButton>\n          )}\n        </div>\n      </div>\n\n      {/* Summary Stats */}\n      <div className=\"grid grid-cols-2 md:grid-cols-4 gap-4\">\n        <StatCard\n          label=\"Total Assets Analyzed\"\n          value={topAssets.length}\n          icon={<Image size={20} />}\n        />\n        <StatCard\n          label=\"High Performers\"\n          value={topAssets.filter((a) => a.engagement_score >= 7).length}\n          icon={<Star size={20} className=\"text-amber-500\" />}\n          variant=\"success\"\n        />\n        <StatCard\n          label=\"Avg Engagement\"\n          value={\n            topAssets.length > 0\n              ? formatEngagementScore(\n                  topAssets.reduce((sum, a) => sum + a.engagement_score, 0) / topAssets.length\n                )\n              : '0'\n          }\n          icon={<TrendingUp size={20} className=\"text-green-500\" />}\n        />\n        <StatCard\n          label=\"Categories\"\n          value={new Set(topAssets.map((a) => a.scene_category).filter(Boolean)).size}\n          icon={<Layers size={20} className=\"text-purple-500\" />}\n        />\n      </div>\n\n      {/* Main Content */}\n      {compact ? (\n        <div className=\"grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-2 gap-4\">\n          <TopAssetsGrid\n            assets={topAssets}\n            onAssetClick={onAssetClick}\n            isLoading={isLoadingTopAssets}\n          />\n          <HeroShotsPanel\n            workspaceId={workspaceId}\n            onAssetClick={onAssetClick}\n          />\n        </div>\n      ) : (\n        <div className=\"grid grid-cols-1 lg:grid-cols-3 gap-4\">\n          {/* Left Column */}\n          <div className=\"lg:col-span-2 space-y-4\">\n            <TopAssetsGrid\n              assets={topAssets}\n              onAssetClick={onAssetClick}\n              isLoading={isLoadingTopAssets}\n            />\n            <HeroShotsPanel\n              workspaceId={workspaceId}\n              onAssetClick={onAssetClick}\n            />\n          </div>\n\n          {/* Right Column */}\n          <div className=\"space-y-4\">\n            <EngagementMetricsPanel assets={topAssets} />\n            <ABTestsList\n              workspaceId={workspaceId}\n              onTestClick={onABTestClick}\n            />\n          </div>\n        </div>\n      )}\n    </div>\n  );\n};\n\nexport default PortfolioRecommendationDashboard;\n"

  "content": "/**\n * A/B Test Panel Component\n *\n * Displays A/B test details, variants, and results for recommendation experiments.\n */\n\nimport React, { useState, useCallback } from 'react';\nimport {\n  Beaker,\n  PlayCircle,\n  PauseCircle,\n  CheckCircle,\n  Clock,\n  X,\n  TrendingUp,\n  Users,\n  Eye,\n  MousePointer,\n  Download,\n  BarChart3,\n  Loader2,\n  Trophy,\n} from 'lucide-react';\nimport { motion } from 'framer-motion';\nimport { AppButton } from '../../ui/AppButton';\nimport { useToast } from '../../ui/Toast';\nimport {\n  useABTest,\n  useABTestResults,\n  useStartABTest,\n  useConcludeABTest,\n} from '../../../hooks/usePortfolioRecommendations';\nimport type { ABTest, ABTestStatus, ABTestVariant } from '../../../services/portfolioRecommendationService';\n\n/* =============================================================================\n   Types\n   ============================================================================= */\n\ninterface ABTestPanelProps {\n  workspaceId: string;\n  testId: string;\n  onClose?: () => void;\n}\n\n/* =============================================================================\n   Helper Functions\n   ============================================================================= */\n\nconst getStatusConfig = (status: ABTestStatus) => {\n  const configs: Record<ABTestStatus, { label: string; color: string; bgColor: string; icon: React.ReactNode }> = {\n    draft: {\n      label: 'Draft',\n      color: 'text-neutral-500',\n      bgColor: 'bg-neutral-500/10',\n      icon: <Clock size={16} />,\n    },\n    running: {\n      label: 'Running',\n      color: 'text-green-500',\n      bgColor: 'bg-green-500/10',\n      icon: <PlayCircle size={16} />,\n    },\n    paused: {\n      label: 'Paused',\n      color: 'text-yellow-500',\n      bgColor: 'bg-yellow-500/10',\n      icon: <PauseCircle size={16} />,\n    },\n    completed: {\n      label: 'Completed',\n      color: 'text-blue-500',\n      bgColor: 'bg-blue-500/10',\n      icon: <CheckCircle size={16} />,\n    },\n    cancelled: {\n      label: 'Cancelled',\n      color: 'text-red-500',\n      bgColor: 'bg-red-500/10',\n      icon: <X size={16} />,\n    },\n  };\n  return configs[status] || configs.draft;\n};\n\nconst formatPercentage = (value: number): string => {\n  return `${(value * 100).toFixed(2)}%`;\n};\n\nconst formatDate = (dateString: string): string => {\n  return new Date(dateString).toLocaleDateString('en-US', {\n    year: 'numeric',\n    month: 'short',\n    day: 'numeric',\n    hour: '2-digit',\n    minute: '2-digit',\n  });\n};\n\n/* =============================================================================\n   Sub Components\n   ============================================================================= */\n\nconst VariantCard: React.FC<{\n  variant: ABTestVariant;\n  index: number;\n  isWinner?: boolean;\n  isControl?: boolean;\n}> = ({ variant, index, isWinner, isControl }) => {\n  const clickRate = variant.impressions > 0\n    ? (variant.clicks / variant.impressions)\n    : 0;\n  const conversionRate = variant.clicks > 0\n    ? (variant.conversions / variant.clicks)\n    : 0;\n\n  return (\n    <motion.div\n      initial={{ opacity: 0, x: -20 }}\n      animate={{ opacity: 1, x: 0 }}\n      transition={{ delay: index * 0.1 }}\n      className={`p-4 rounded-lg border ${\n        isWinner\n          ? 'border-green-500/50 bg-green-500/5'\n          : 'border-border bg-surface-tertiary'\n      }`}\n    >\n      <div className=\"flex items-center justify-between mb-3\">\n        <div className=\"flex items-center gap-2\">\n          <span className={`w-8 h-8 rounded-full flex items-center justify-center font-bold text-sm ${\n            isWinner\n              ? 'bg-green-500 text-white'\n              : 'bg-primary/10 text-primary'\n          }`}>\n            {String.fromCharCode(65 + index)}\n          </span>\n          <div>\n            <p className=\"font-medium text-text-primary\">{variant.name}</p>\n            {isControl && (\n              <span className=\"text-xs text-text-tertiary\">Control</span>\n            )}\n          </div>\n        </div>\n        {isWinner && (\n          <div className=\"flex items-center gap-1 px-2 py-1 rounded-full bg-green-500/20 text-green-500 text-xs font-medium\">\n            <Trophy size={12} />\n            Winner\n          </div>\n        )}\n      </div>\n\n      {variant.description && (\n        <p className=\"text-sm text-text-secondary mb-3\">{variant.description}</p>\n      )}\n\n      <div className=\"grid grid-cols-2 gap-3\">\n        <div className=\"p-2 rounded bg-surface-secondary\">\n          <div className=\"flex items-center gap-1 text-text-tertiary text-xs mb-1\">\n            <Eye size={12} />\n            Impressions\n          </div>\n          <p className=\"text-lg font-bold text-text-primary\">\n            {variant.impressions.toLocaleString()}\n          </p>\n        </div>\n        <div className=\"p-2 rounded bg-surface-secondary\">\n          <div className=\"flex items-center gap-1 text-text-tertiary text-xs mb-1\">\n            <MousePointer size={12} />\n            Clicks\n          </div>\n          <p className=\"text-lg font-bold text-text-primary\">\n            {variant.clicks.toLocaleString()}\n          </p>\n        </div>\n        <div className=\"p-2 rounded bg-surface-secondary\">\n          <div className=\"flex items-center gap-1 text-text-tertiary text-xs mb-1\">\n            <TrendingUp size={12} />\n            Click Rate\n          </div>\n          <p className=\"text-lg font-bold text-text-primary\">\n            {formatPercentage(clickRate)}\n          </p>\n        </div>\n        <div className=\"p-2 rounded bg-surface-secondary\">\n          <div className=\"flex items-center gap-1 text-text-tertiary text-xs mb-1\">\n            <Download size={12} />\n            Conversions\n          </div>\n          <p className=\"text-lg font-bold text-text-primary\">\n            {variant.conversions.toLocaleString()}\n          </p>\n        </div>\n      </div>\n\n      <div className=\"mt-3 pt-3 border-t border-border\">\n        <div className=\"flex items-center justify-between text-sm\">\n          <span className=\"text-text-tertiary\">Conversion Rate</span>\n          <span className={`font-medium ${isWinner ? 'text-green-500' : 'text-text-primary'}`}>\n            {formatPercentage(conversionRate)}\n          </span>\n        </div>\n      </div>\n    </motion.div>\n  );\n};\n\n/* =============================================================================\n   Main Component\n   ============================================================================= */\n\nexport const ABTestPanel: React.FC<ABTestPanelProps> = ({\n  workspaceId,\n  testId,\n  onClose,\n}) => {\n  const { addToast } = useToast();\n\n  // Queries\n  const { data: test, isLoading: isLoadingTest } = useABTest(workspaceId, testId);\n  const { data: results, isLoading: isLoadingResults } = useABTestResults(\n    workspaceId,\n    testId,\n    { enabled: !!test && test.status !== 'draft' }\n  );\n\n  // Mutations\n  const { mutate: startTest, isPending: isStarting } = useStartABTest(workspaceId);\n  const { mutate: concludeTest, isPending: isConcluding } = useConcludeABTest(workspaceId);\n\n  // Handlers\n  const handleStart = useCallback(() => {\n    startTest(testId, {\n      onSuccess: () => {\n        addToast({ variant: 'success', message: 'Test started successfully' });\n      },\n      onError: (error) => {\n        addToast({\n          variant: 'error',\n          message: error instanceof Error ? error.message : 'Failed to start test',\n        });\n      },\n    });\n  }, [startTest, testId, addToast]);\n\n  const handleConclude = useCallback((winnerVariantId?: string) => {\n    concludeTest(\n      { testId, winnerVariant: winnerVariantId },\n      {\n        onSuccess: () => {\n          addToast({ variant: 'success', message: 'Test concluded successfully' });\n        },\n        onError: (error) => {\n          addToast({\n            variant: 'error',\n            message: error instanceof Error ? error.message : 'Failed to conclude test',\n          });\n        },\n      }\n    );\n  }, [concludeTest, testId, addToast]);\n\n  if (isLoadingTest) {\n    return (\n      <div className=\"bg-surface-secondary rounded-xl border border-border p-8\">\n        <div className=\"flex items-center justify-center\">\n          <Loader2 size={32} className=\"animate-spin text-primary\" />\n        </div>\n      </div>\n    );\n  }\n\n  if (!test) {\n    return (\n      <div className=\"bg-surface-secondary rounded-xl border border-border p-8 text-center\">\n        <Beaker size={48} className=\"mx-auto mb-4 text-text-tertiary opacity-50\" />\n        <p className=\"text-text-tertiary\">Test not found</p>\n      </div>\n    );\n  }\n\n  const statusConfig = getStatusConfig(test.status);\n\n  return (\n    <div className=\"bg-surface-secondary rounded-xl border border-border overflow-hidden\">\n      {/* Header */}\n      <div className=\"p-4 border-b border-border\">\n        <div className=\"flex items-start justify-between\">\n          <div>\n            <div className=\"flex items-center gap-2 mb-1\">\n              <Beaker size={20} className=\"text-purple-500\" />\n              <h3 className=\"font-semibold text-text-primary\">{test.name}</h3>\n              <span className={`px-2 py-0.5 rounded text-xs flex items-center gap-1 ${statusConfig.bgColor} ${statusConfig.color}`}>\n                {statusConfig.icon}\n                {statusConfig.label}\n              </span>\n            </div>\n            {test.description && (\n              <p className=\"text-sm text-text-tertiary\">{test.description}</p>\n            )}\n          </div>\n          {onClose && (\n            <button\n              onClick={onClose}\n              className=\"p-1 rounded hover:bg-surface-tertiary transition-colors\"\n            >\n              <X size={20} className=\"text-text-tertiary\" />\n            </button>\n          )}\n        </div>\n\n        {/* Meta info */}\n        <div className=\"flex gap-4 mt-3 text-xs text-text-tertiary\">\n          <span>Created: {formatDate(test.created_at)}</span>\n          {test.started_at && <span>Started: {formatDate(test.started_at)}</span>}\n          {test.concluded_at && <span>Concluded: {formatDate(test.concluded_at)}</span>}\n        </div>\n      </div>\n\n      {/* Variants */}\n      <div className=\"p-4\">\n        <h4 className=\"text-sm font-medium text-text-primary mb-3 flex items-center gap-2\">\n          <BarChart3 size={16} className=\"text-primary\" />\n          Variants ({test.variants.length})\n        </h4>\n        <div className=\"grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-2 gap-4\">\n          {test.variants.map((variant, index) => (\n            <VariantCard\n              key={variant.variant_id}\n              variant={variant}\n              index={index}\n              isWinner={test.winner_variant_id === variant.variant_id}\n              isControl={variant.is_control}\n            />\n          ))}\n        </div>\n      </div>\n\n      {/* Statistical Results */}\n      {results && (\n        <div className=\"p-4 border-t border-border\">\n          <h4 className=\"text-sm font-medium text-text-primary mb-3 flex items-center gap-2\">\n            <TrendingUp size={16} className=\"text-green-500\" />\n            Statistical Analysis\n          </h4>\n          <div className=\"grid grid-cols-2 md:grid-cols-4 gap-3\">\n            <div className=\"p-3 rounded-lg bg-surface-tertiary\">\n              <p className=\"text-xs text-text-tertiary mb-1\">Total Impressions</p>\n              <p className=\"text-xl font-bold text-text-primary\">\n                {results.total_impressions.toLocaleString()}\n              </p>\n            </div>\n            <div className=\"p-3 rounded-lg bg-surface-tertiary\">\n              <p className=\"text-xs text-text-tertiary mb-1\">Total Clicks</p>\n              <p className=\"text-xl font-bold text-text-primary\">\n                {results.total_clicks.toLocaleString()}\n              </p>\n            </div>\n            <div className=\"p-3 rounded-lg bg-surface-tertiary\">\n              <p className=\"text-xs text-text-tertiary mb-1\">P-Value</p>\n              <p className={`text-xl font-bold ${\n                results.p_value < 0.05 ? 'text-green-500' : 'text-text-primary'\n              }`}>\n                {results.p_value.toFixed(4)}\n              </p>\n            </div>\n            <div className=\"p-3 rounded-lg bg-surface-tertiary\">\n              <p className=\"text-xs text-text-tertiary mb-1\">Statistically Significant</p>\n              <p className={`text-xl font-bold ${\n                results.is_significant ? 'text-green-500' : 'text-yellow-500'\n              }`}>\n                {results.is_significant ? 'Yes' : 'No'}\n              </p>\n            </div>\n          </div>\n          {results.recommended_winner && (\n            <div className=\"mt-3 p-3 rounded-lg bg-green-500/10 border border-green-500/30\">\n              <p className=\"text-sm text-green-500 flex items-center gap-2\">\n                <Trophy size={16} />\n                Recommended Winner: {test.variants.find(v => v.variant_id === results.recommended_winner)?.name || 'Unknown'}\n              </p>\n            </div>\n          )}\n        </div>\n      )}\n\n      {/* Actions */}\n      <div className=\"p-4 border-t border-border flex items-center justify-between\">\n        <div className=\"text-xs text-text-tertiary\">\n          {test.status === 'running' && (\n            <span>Collecting data...</span>\n          )}\n        </div>\n        <div className=\"flex gap-2\">\n          {test.status === 'draft' && (\n            <AppButton\n              variant=\"primary\"\n              size=\"sm\"\n              leftIcon={isStarting ? <Loader2 size={14} className=\"animate-spin\" /> : <PlayCircle size={14} />}\n              onClick={handleStart}\n              disabled={isStarting}\n            >\n              Start Test\n            </AppButton>\n          )}\n          {test.status === 'running' && (\n            <AppButton\n              variant=\"secondary\"\n              size=\"sm\"\n              leftIcon={isConcluding ? <Loader2 size={14} className=\"animate-spin\" /> : <CheckCircle size={14} />}\n              onClick={() => handleConclude(results?.recommended_winner)}\n              disabled={isConcluding}\n            >\n              Conclude Test\n            </AppButton>\n          )}\n        </div>\n      </div>\n    </div>\n  );\n};\n\nexport default ABTestPanel;\n"
}

| 'feedback_detailed';\n\n// Event metadata interface\nexport interface RecommendationEventMetadata {\n  recommendationId?: string;\n  recommendationType?: RecommendationType;\n  itemId?: string;\n   recommendationId: string,\n    itemId: string,\n    assetId: string,\n    rank: number\n  ) => void;\n  trackItemSelected: (\n    recommendationId: string,\n    itemId: string,\n    assetId: =============================================================================\n   Hook Implementation\n   ============================================================================= */\n\nexport function useRecommendationTracking(\n  options: UseRecommendationTrackingOptions\n): RecommendationTrackingResult {\n  const {\n    workspaceId,\n    sessionId,\n    visitorId,\n    enableBatching = false,\n    batchSize = 10,\n    batchIntervalMs = 5000,\n  } = options;\n\n  // Event queue for batching\n  const eventQueue = useRef<RecordEventRequest[]>([]);\n  const batchTimerRef = useRef<ReturnType<typeof setTimeout> | null>(null);\n\n  // Build base event request\n  const buildEventRequest = useCallback(\n    (\n      eventType: RecommendationEventType,\n      eventCategory: RecommendationEventCategory,\n      metadata?: RecommendationEventMetadata\n    ): RecordEventRequest => ({\n      eventType,\n      eventCategory,\n      galleryId: metadata?.galleryId,\n   testId: metadata?.testId,\n        variantId: metadata?.variantId,\n        sceneCategory: metadata?.sceneCategory,\n        rank: metadata?.rank,\n        totalItems: metadata?.totalItems,\n        feedbackRating: metadata?.feedbackRating,\n        feedbackComment: metadata?.feedbackComment,\n        feedbackSource: metadata?.feedbackSource,\n        ...metadata?.context,\n      },\n    }),\n    [sessionId, visitorId]\n  );\n\n  // Flush queued events\n  const flushEvents = useCallback(async (): Promise<void> => {\n    if (eventQueue.current.length === 0) return;\n\n    const eventsToSend = [...eventQueue.current];\n    eventQueue.current = [];\n\n    if (batchTimerRef.current) {\n      clearTimeout(batchTimerRef.current);\n      batchTimerRef.current = null;\n    }\n\n    try {\n      await analyticsService.recordEventsBatch(workspaceId, eventsToSend);\n    } catch (error) {\n      // Re-queue failed events\n      eventQueue.current = [...eventsToSend, ...eventQueue.current];\n      console.error('Failed to flush recommendation events:', error);\n    }\n  }, [workspaceId]);\n\n  // Send or queue an event\n  const sendEvent = useCallback(\n    async (event: RecordEventRequest): Promise<void> => {\n      if (enableBatching) {\n        eventQueue.current.push(event);\n\n        if (eventQueue.current.length >= batchSize) {\n          await flushEvents();\n        } else if (!batchTimerRef.current) {\n          batchTimerRef.current = setTimeout(() => {\n            flushEvents();\n          }, batchIntervalMs);\n        }\n      } else {\n        try {\n          await analyticsService.recordEvent(workspaceId, event);\n        } catch (error) {\n          console.error('Failed to record recommendation event:', error);\n        }\n      }\n    },\n    [workspaceId, enableBatching, batchSize, batchIntervalMs, flushEvents]\n  );\n\n  // Core tracking method\n  const trackEvent = useCallback(\n    (\n      eventType: RecommendationEventType,\n      metadata?: RecommendationEventMetadata\n    ): void => {\n      const category = getEventCategory(eventType);\n      const event = buildEventRequest(eventType, category, metadata);\n      sendEvent(event);\n    },\n    [buildEventRequest, sendEvent]\n  );\n\n  // Recommendation tracking methods\n  const trackRecommendationGenerated = useCallback(\n    (\n      recommendationId: string,\n      type:
