recommendationType: type,\n        totalItems: itemCount,\n        galleryId,\n        clientId,\n      });\n    },\n    [trackEvent]\n  );\n\n  const trackRecommendationViewed = useCallback(\n    (recommendationId: string, type: RecommendationType): void => {\n      trackEvent('recommendation_viewed', {\n        recommendationId,\n        recommendationType: type,\n      });\n    },\n    [trackEvent]\n  );\n\n  const trackRecommendationAccepted = useCallback(\n    (\n      recommendationId: string,\n      type: RecommendationType,\n      acceptedCount: number,\n      totalCount: number\n    ): void => {\n      trackEvent('recommendation_accepted', {\n        recommendationId,\n        recommendationType: type,\n        totalItems: totalCount,\n        context: { acceptedCount },\n      });\n    },\n    [trackEvent]\n  );\n\n  const trackRecommendationRejected = useCallback(\n    (recommendationId: string, type: RecommendationType): void => {\n      trackEvent('recommendation_rejected', {\n        recommendationId,\n        recommendationType: type,\n      });\n    },\n    [trackEvent]\n  );\n\n  // Item tracking methods\n  const trackItemViewed = useCallback(\n    (\n      recommendationId: string,\n      itemId: string,\n      assetId: string,\n      rank: number,\n      viewDurationMs?: number\n    ): void => {\n      trackEvent('recommendation_item_viewed', {\n        recommendationId,\n        itemId,\n        assetId,\n        rank,\n        viewDurationMs,\n      });\n    },\n    [trackEvent]\n  );\n\n  const trackItemClicked = useCallback(\n    (\n      recommendationId: string,\n      itemId: string,\n      assetId: string,\n      rank: number\n    ): void => {\n      trackEvent('recommendation_item_clicked', {\n        recommendationId,\n        itemId,\n        assetId,\n        rank,\n      });\n    },\n    [trackEvent]\n  );\n\n  const trackItemSelected = useCallback(\n    (recommendationId: string, itemId: string, assetId: string): void => {\n      trackEvent('recommendation_item_selected', {\n        recommendationId,\n        itemId,\n        assetId,\n      });\n    },\n    [trackEvent]\n  );\n\n  const trackItemRejected = useCallback(\n    (recommendationId: string, itemId: string, assetId: string): void => {\n      trackEvent('recommendation_item_rejected', {\n        recommendationId,\n        itemId,\n        assetId,\n      });\n    },\n    [trackEvent]\n  );\n\n  const trackItemDownloaded = useCallback(\n    (recommendationId: string, itemId: string, assetId: string): void => {\n      trackEvent('recommendation_item_downloaded', {\n        recommendationId,\n        itemId,\n        assetId,\n      });\n    },\n    [trackEvent]\n  );\n\n  // A/B test tracking methods\n  const trackABTestCreated = useCallback(\n    (testId: string, variantCount: number): void => {\n      trackEvent('ab_test_created', {\n        testId,\n        context: { variantCount },\n      });\n    },\n    [trackEvent]\n  );\n\n  const trackABTestStarted = useCallback(\n    (testId: string): void => {\n      trackEvent('ab_test_started', { testId });\n    },\n    [trackEvent]\n  );\n\n  const trackABTestConcluded = useCallback(\n    (testId: string, winnerVariantId?: string): void => {\n      trackEvent('ab_test_concluded', {\n        testId,\n        variantId: winnerVariantId,\n      });\n    },\n    [trackEvent]\n  );\n\n  const trackABTestImpression = useCallback(\n    (testId: string, variantId: string): void => {\n      trackEvent('ab_test_impression', { testId, variantId });\n    },\n    [trackEvent]\n  );\n\n  const trackABTestClick = useCallback(\n    (testId: string, variantId: string): void => {\n      trackEvent('ab_test_click', { testId, variantId });\n    },\n    [trackEvent]\n  );\n\n  const trackABTestConversion = useCallback(\n    (testId: string, variantId: string): void => {\n      trackEvent('ab_test_conversion', { testId, variantId });\n    },\n    [trackEvent]\n  );\n\n  // Feedback tracking\n  const trackFeedback = useCallback(\n    (\n      recommendationId: string,\n      rating: 'positive' | 'negative',\n      source?: string,\n      comment?: string\n    ): void => {\n      const eventType = rating === 'positive' ? 'feedback_positive' : 'feedback_negative';\n      trackEvent(eventType, {\n        recommendationId,\n        feedbackRating: rating,\n        feedbackSource: source,\n        feedbackComment: comment,\n      });\n    },\n    [trackEvent]\n  );\n\n  return {\n    trackEvent,\n    trackRecommendationGenerated,\n    trackRecommendationViewed,\n    trackRecommendationAccepted,\n    trackRecommendationRejected,\n    trackItemViewed,\n    trackItemClicked,\n    trackItemSelected,\n    trackItemRejected,\n    trackItemDownloaded,\n    trackABTestCreated,\n    trackABTestStarted,\n    trackABTestConcluded,\n    trackABTestImpression,\n    trackABTestClick,\n    trackABTestConversion,\n    trackFeedback,\n    flushEvents,\n  };\n}\n\n/* =============================================================================\n   Helper Functions\n   ============================================================================= */\n\nfunction getEventCategory(eventType: RecommendationEventType): RecommendationEventCategory {\n  if (eventType.startsWith('recommendation_item_')) {\n    return 'recommendation_item';\n  }\n  if (eventType.startsWith('ab_test_')) {\n    return 'recommendation_ab_test';\n  }\n  if (eventType.startsWith('feedback_')) {\n    return 'recommendation_feedback';\n  }\n  return 'recommendation';\n}\n\nexport default useRecommendationTracking;\n"
}

The AI Portfolio Recommendation Engine feature is now complete. Here's a summary of what was implemented:

## Summary: AI Portfolio Recommendation Engine

### Backend (Previously Completed)
- **Database Migration** (`0185_portfolio_recommendation_engine.py`) - Tables for recommendations, items, client preferences, A/B tests
- **Pydantic Schemas** (`portfolio_recommendation.py`) - Request/response models
- **Repository** (`portfolio_recommendation_repository.py`) - Data access layer with vector similarity search
- **Service** (`portfolio_recommendation_service.py`) - Business logic with CLIP embeddings, engagement scoring
- **API Endpoints** (`portfolio_recommendations.py`) - Full REST API registered at `/api/v1/workspaces/{workspace_id}/portfolio-recommendations`

### Frontend (Completed in This Session)

**Services & Hooks:**
- `portfolioRecommendationService.ts` - TypeScript API client with full type definitions
- `usePortfolioRecommendations.ts` - React Query hooks for all operations
- `useRecommendationTracking.ts` - Analytics event tracking hook with batching support

**Components** (`frontend/src/components/features/portfolio-recommendations/`):
- `PortfolioRecommendationDashboard.tsx` - Main dashboard with top assets, hero shots, A/B tests, engagement metrics
- `RecommendationCard.tsx` - Individual recommendation display with items grid, feedback buttons
- `RecommendationGenerator.tsx` - Form to generate new recommendations with quick/advanced modes
- `ABTestPanel.tsx` - A/B test details with variant cards, statistical analysis
- `index.ts` - Barrel exports for all components, hooks, and types

### Key Features
1. **CLIP-based Visual Similarity** - Find similar photos using 512-dim embeddings with pgvector HNSW indexing
2. **Engagement Scoring** - Multi-factor scoring (views, downloads, favorites, dwell time)
3. **Personalized Recommendations** - Based on client preferences and interaction history
4. **Seasonal/Contextual Recommendations** - Theme-based suggestions
5. **A/B Testing** - Experiment with different recommendation strategies
6. **Real-time Analytics Tracking** - Event batching for performance
